                    var redirectTo = (gotoHomepage ? "" : "Ticket/Display.html?id=371402");
                    var dialogContent = '<div id="manualReconcileTicketDialog" title="Manual Match"> 			<form method="post" id="manualReconcileTicketForm" action="/FireFlow/Elements/DoManualReconcile" enctype="multipart/form-data"> 			    <input type="hidden" name="TicketStatus" value="' + newStatus + '" /> 			    <input type="hidden" name="RedirectTo" value="' + redirectTo + '" /> 				<table> 			        <tr> 			        	<td class="labeltop">Change Request:</td> 			            <td class="value">371402: Access between Staff UAT Range to Oracle DB RAC Cluster for PIMS Access - rForex PREPROD</td> 			        	<input type="hidden" name="TicketId" value="371402" /> 			        </tr> 			        <tr> 			            <td class="labeltop">Change Id:</td> 			        	<td><input dojoType="dijit.form.TextBox" type="text" name="ChangeId" value="" /></td> 			        </tr> 			        <tr> 			        	<td class="labeltop">FireFlow Notes:</td> 			            <td class="value">Manual match.</td> 			        	<input type="hidden" name="FireFlowNotes" value="Manual match." /> 			        </tr> 			        <tr> 			        	<td class="labeltop">User Notes:</td> 			        	<td><textarea dojoType="dijit.form.SimpleTextarea" name="UserNotes" 			        		 style="width:40em; max-height:57px; min-height:57px; height:57px;"></textarea></td> 			        </tr> 			    </table> 			</form> 		</div>';

                    jQuery('body').append(dialogContent);

                    jQuery("#manualReconcileTicketDialog").dialog({
                        dialogClass: "dialogWithCloseButton",
                        modal: true,
                        autoOpen: false,
                        resizable: false,
                        width: 685,
                        closeOnEscape: true,
                        close: function() {
                            jQuery(this).remove();
                        },
                        buttons: [{
                            text: FFLoc('Cancel'),
                            click: function() {
                                jQuery(this).dialog("close");
                            }
                        }, {
                            text: FFLoc('OK'),
                            click: function() {
                                jQuery("#manualReconcileTicketForm").submit();
                            }
                        }]
                    });

                    jQuery("#manualReconcileTicketDialog").dialog('open');
                }
            </script>

                                    <script>
                                        jQuery(document).one('almostReady', function() {
                                            jQuery("#ey8lt5beButton").click(function(event, nonInteractiveMode) {
                                                // Toggle the led light
                                                jQuery(this).toggleClass("LedOn");

                                                // Bad IE8, Bad!!
                                                // Due to IE8 in-ability to identify when to redraw on class changes (that we do above)
                                                // we hide/show the button to force redraw.
                                                // Compatability note: We use jQuery.browser here, which is unsupported on jQuery 1.9 and above.
                                                // hopefully by the time we upgrade to 1.10/2.0 we'll drop support for IE8. hopefully... if not, 
                                                // we'll need to use an html feature detection library like http://modernizr.com instead.
                                                if (jQuery.browser.msie && parseInt(jQuery.browser.version, 10) === 8 || jQuery.browser.msie && parseInt(jQuery.browser.version, 10) === 7) {
                                                    jQuery(this).hide();
                                                    jQuery(this).show();
                                                }

                                                // If one of the buttons is lit (has class LedOn):
                                                // Add space from top to below the buttons, so the panels will show 
                                                // under the buttons, and not behind them.
                                                if (jQuery(this).add(jQuery(this).siblings()).hasClass("LedOn")) {
                                                    jQuery("div.RequestPanels").addClass("PanelBelowButtons", 400);
                                                } else {
                                                    jQuery("div.RequestPanels").removeClass("PanelBelowButtons", 400);
                                                }

                                                // Move this panel up (only in interactive mode)
                                                if (jQuery(this).hasClass("LedOn")) {
                                                    if (nonInteractiveMode == undefined || nonInteractiveMode == false) {
                                                        jQuery("#ey8lt5bePanel").prependTo(jQuery("#ey8lt5bePanel").parent());
                                                    }
                                                }
                                                jQuery("#ey8lt5bePanel").slideToggle(400);
                                            });

                                        });
                                    </script>

                        <script type="text/javascript">
                            jQuery('#implementationInProgress').hide();
                            jQuery('#implementationComplete').show();

                            function notifyUponImplementationComplete() {
                                window.setTimeout(function() {
                                    var message = "Implementation has completed on some of the sub-tickets.\nDo you wish to refresh the page now?";
                                    if (FFJSU.refreshDialogOpen) {
                                        return;
                                    }
                                    FFJSU.refreshDialogOpen = true;
                                    if (window.confirm(message)) {
                                        window.location.replace(window.location.href);
                                    } else {
                                        FFJSU.refreshDialogOpen = false;
                                    }
                                }, 1000);
                            }
                        </script>

                        <script type="text/javascript">
                            window.StatusModificationCheck = {
                                shouldMonitor: true
                            };

                            var ticketId = "371402";
                            var ticketStatusInitial = "implement";

                            jQuery(document).on("monitorTicketStatus", function() {
                                window.StatusModificationCheck.shouldMonitor = true;
                                monitorTicketStatusChange()
                            });
                            jQuery(document).on("stopMonitorTicketStatus", function() {
                                window.StatusModificationCheck.shouldMonitor = false;
                            });

                            function monitorTicketStatusChange() {
                                CommandsDispatcher.dispatchCommand('Tickets', 'getCurrentTicketStatus', {
                                    "ticketId": ticketId
                                }, function(response) {
                                    try {
                                        outputObject = JSON.parse(response);
                                        var ticketStatus = outputObject.data;
                                        if (window.StatusModificationCheck.shouldMonitor) {
                                            if (ticketStatus === ticketStatusInitial) {
                                                window.setTimeout(monitorTicketStatusChange, 10 * 1000);
                                            } else {
                                                jQuery(document).trigger("ticketStatusChanged");
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } catch (e) {
                                    }
                                });
                            }
                        </script>
                        <script type="text/javascript">
                            var shouldChangeStatusWithoutConfirmation = false;

                            function notifyUponStatusChange() {
                                //if this is multiDeviceObjectWorkflow - refresh the page when result is ready 
                                shouldChangeStatusWithoutConfirmation = jQuery('#MultiDeviceObjectDetails').length !== 0 ? true : shouldChangeStatusWithoutConfirmation;
                                // Status changed, but wait 5 seconds more before asking to let others refresh the 
                                // page intentionally (like in work-order)
                                window.setTimeout(function() {
                                    var message = "The status of the change request has changed.\nDo you wish to refresh the page now?";
                                    if (FFJSU.refreshDialogOpen) {
                                        return;
                                    }
                                    FFJSU.refreshDialogOpen = true;
                                    if (shouldChangeStatusWithoutConfirmation || window.confirm(message)) {
                                        var url = "/FireFlow/Ticket/Display.html";
                                        url = addGETParamToURL(url, "id", "371402")
                                        window.location.replace(url);
                                    } else {
                                        FFJSU.refreshDialogOpen = false;
                                    }
                                }, 5000);
                            }

                            jQuery(document).on("ticketStatusChanged", notifyUponStatusChange);
                            jQuery(document).trigger("monitorTicketStatus");
                        </script>
                        <script type="text/javascript">
                            function showHideBlankMessage() {
                                // Iterate on all tags below the body (ignoring the notifications and the empty message boxes). If any of those have actual height we should not
                                // display the empty message.
                                var contentExistInPage = false;
                                jQuery("div#body > *:visible") // All visible tags under div#body
                                .not("div.results") // Ignore the results tag (notifications)
                                .not("a#skipnav") // Ignore whatever it is
                                .each(function() {
                                    if (jQuery(this).height() > 0) {
                                        contentExistInPage = true;
                                    }
                                });

                                //If there is no content click on the object/traffic/rule button to open collapsed panel
                                if (!contentExistInPage && jQuery("div.RequestPanels>div").filter(":visible").length == 0) {
                                    if (jQuery("div.TrafficCollapsiblePanel.collapsiblePanelButton").length == 0) {
                                        jQuery("div.DetailsCollapsiblePanel.collapsiblePanelButton").click();
                                    } else {
                                        jQuery("div.TrafficCollapsiblePanel.collapsiblePanelButton").click();
                                    }

                                    // the buttons actions are set in 'almostReady' and this method is called before so in the first time the button click will do nothing
                                    if (jQuery("div.RequestPanels>div").filter(":visible").length == 0) {
                                        window.setTimeout(showHideBlankMessage, 500);
                                    }
                                }

                            }

                            // Set initial state of the "IntentionallyBlank" message box
                            showHideBlankMessage();
                        </script>
